spec_pledge_make = {
	set_variable = spec_pledge
}

spec_pledge_withdraw = {
	set_variable = { name = spec_pledge_leave value = 24 }
}

spec_charter_to_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_known
			has_variable = spec_centre_trust
		}
		if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
		set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust multiply = 0.75 } }
		if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_charter_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = spec_charter_lock days = 3650 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
		spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
		spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
		spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
		spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
	}
}

	}

		spec_pursue_stop_price = yes

spec_pursue_to_price = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_pw_sum } }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_price value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_harbour value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_companies value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_futures value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_lender value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_railway value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_floor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_sound value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_pockets value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_door value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_sum value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n value = 0 }
	}
	change_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n add = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_price value = var:spec_pursue_n }
	set_variable = spec_has_price
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lock_price value = 60 }
}

spec_pursue_stop_price = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_harbour
			var:spec_pu_harbour > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_companies
			var:spec_pu_companies > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_futures
			var:spec_pu_futures > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_lender
			var:spec_pu_lender > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_railway
			var:spec_pu_railway > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_floor
			var:spec_pu_floor > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_sound
			var:spec_pu_sound > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_pockets
			var:spec_pu_pockets > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_door
			var:spec_pu_door > var:spec_pu_price
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_door add = -1 }
	}
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pw_price value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_price_0 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_price_0
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_price_1 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_price_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_price_2 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_price_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_price_3 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_price_3
	}
	remove_variable = spec_pu_price
	remove_variable = spec_has_price
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lock_price }
		remove_variable = spec_pu_lock_price
	}
	change_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n add = -1 }
}

spec_pursue_to_harbour = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_pw_sum } }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_price value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_harbour value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_companies value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_futures value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_lender value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_railway value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_floor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_sound value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_pockets value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_door value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pw_sum value = 0 }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n value = 0 }
	}
	change_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n add = 1 }
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = var:spec_pursue_n }
	set_variable = spec_has_harbour
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lock_harbour value = 60 }
}

spec_pursue_stop_harbour = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_price
			var:spec_pu_price > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_price add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_companies
			var:spec_pu_companies > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_futures
			var:spec_pu_futures > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_lender
			var:spec_pu_lender > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_railway
			var:spec_pu_railway > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_floor
			var:spec_pu_floor > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_sound
			var:spec_pu_sound > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_pockets
			var:spec_pu_pockets > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_door
			var:spec_pu_door > var:spec_pu_harbour
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_door add = -1 }
	}
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pw_harbour value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_harbour_0 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_harbour_0
	}

			var:spec_pu_futures > var:spec_pu_sound
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_lender
			var:spec_pu_lender > var:spec_pu_sound
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_railway
			var:spec_pu_railway > var:spec_pu_sound
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway add = -1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_pu_floor
			var:spec_pu_floor > var:spec_pu_sound
		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor add = -1 }
	}
	if = {

			has_variable = spec_pu_pockets

	if = {

			has_variable = spec_pu_door

		}
		change_variable = { name = spec_pu_door add = -1 }
	}
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pw_sound value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_0 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_0
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_1 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_2 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_3 }
		remove_modifier = spec_pursue_sound_3
	}
	remove_variable = spec_pu_sound
	remove_variable = spec_has_sound
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lock_sound }
		remove_variable = spec_pu_lock_sound
	}
	change_variable = { name = spec_pursue_n add = -1 }
}

		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = var:spec_pu_price }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies var:spec_pu_companies = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies value = var:spec_pu_price }

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures var:spec_pu_futures = local_var:spec_to }

		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = var:spec_pu_price }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor value = var:spec_pu_price }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = var:spec_pu_price }
	}

		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender var:spec_pu_lender = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_door var:spec_pu_door = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_door value = var:spec_pu_pockets }
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets value = local_var:spec_to }
}

spec_pursue_left_door = {
	set_local_variable = { name = spec_to value = var:spec_pu_door }
	change_local_variable = { name = spec_to add = -1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price var:spec_pu_price = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_price value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour var:spec_pu_harbour = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies var:spec_pu_companies = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures var:spec_pu_futures = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender var:spec_pu_lender = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender value = var:spec_pu_door }

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = var:spec_pu_door }

	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = local_var:spec_to }

	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_pockets var:spec_pu_pockets = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_door value = local_var:spec_to }
}

spec_pursue_right_door = {
	set_local_variable = { name = spec_to value = var:spec_pu_door }
	change_local_variable = { name = spec_to add = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price var:spec_pu_price = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_price value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour var:spec_pu_harbour = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies var:spec_pu_companies = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures var:spec_pu_futures = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender var:spec_pu_lender = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_pockets var:spec_pu_pockets = local_var:spec_to }
		set_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets value = var:spec_pu_door }
	}
	set_variable = { name = spec_pu_door value = local_var:spec_to }
}

# the Financial Centres (centres.py): once a month, for the whole world
spec_rank_centres = {
	# the Exchange score's yearly average and the world's pool income per person (centres.py)
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_world_inc value = 0 }
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_world_pop value = 0 }
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_xs_inc } }
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_inc value = spec_pool_month }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_inc value = { value = spec_pool_month subtract = var:spec_xs_inc multiply = 0.1 add = var:spec_xs_inc } }
		}
		change_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_world_inc add = var:spec_xs_inc }
		change_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_world_pop add = { value = total_population divide = 1000000 } }
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_world_pc value = { value = global_var:spec_xs_world_inc divide = { value = global_var:spec_xs_world_pop min = 1 } } }
	every_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_conf }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_calm } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_calm value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_calm }
					set_variable = { name = spec_centre_calm value = var:spec_calm }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_trust } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_calm min = 60 max = 120 } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_player = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_review }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_change value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = var:spec_trust_review } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_goods_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_goods_pending } value = var:spec_trust_goods_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_panics_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_panics_pending } value = var:spec_trust_panics_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_lending_pending } value = var:spec_trust_lending_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_debts_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_debts_pending } value = var:spec_trust_debts_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_rivals_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_rivals_pending } value = var:spec_trust_rivals_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_chips_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_chips_pending } value = var:spec_trust_chips_pending } } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_trust_charter_last value = { value = 0 if = { limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_charter_pending } value = var:spec_trust_charter_pending } } }
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_goods_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_panics_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_debts_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_rivals_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_chips_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_charter_pending value = 0 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_trust_review value = var:spec_centre_trust }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_trust_review }
				remove_variable = spec_trust_review
				remove_variable = spec_trust_change
				remove_variable = spec_trust_goods_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_goods_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_panics_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_panics_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_lending_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_lending_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_debts_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_debts_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_rivals_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_rivals_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_chips_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_chips_last
				remove_variable = spec_trust_charter_pending
				remove_variable = spec_trust_charter_last
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_panic } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_lax var:spec_lax >= 0.8 }
					if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = { value = 0.0 multiply = spec_exch_loss } min = 0 } }
					if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_crunch } OR = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_lax } var:spec_lax < 0.6 } }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_lax var:spec_lax < 0.2 }
							if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
							set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust add = 2.4 max = 120 } }
							if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_lax var:spec_lax < 0.4 }
							if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
							set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust add = 1.8 max = 120 } }
							if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
					}
					else = {
						if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
						set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust add = 1.2 max = 120 } }
						if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_lending_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
					}
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_sound value = { value = spec_log_ksnd add = 1 } }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_pledge
					in_default = yes
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = 0 }
				spec_cool_conf = { AMOUNT = 15 }
				add_modifier = { name = spec_pledge_broken months = 60 }
				set_variable = { name = spec_pledge_broken value = { value = spec_log_pbroke add = 1 } }
				remove_variable = spec_pledge
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pledge_leave }
					remove_variable = spec_pledge_leave
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes }
					post_notification = spec_pledge_broken
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_pledge_leave }
				change_variable = { name = spec_pledge_leave add = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { var:spec_pledge_leave <= 0 }
					remove_variable = spec_pledge_leave
					remove_variable = spec_pledge
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_player = no }
				if = {
					limit = {
						spec_can_pledge = yes
						in_default = no
						scaled_debt < 0.3
						gold_reserves >= spec_rescue_cost
					}
					spec_pledge_make = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						spec_can_pledge_leave = yes
						OR = {
							scaled_debt > 0.4
							gold_reserves < spec_rescue_cost
						}
					}
					spec_pledge_withdraw = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_price
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_price
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_harbour
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_harbour
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_companies
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_companies
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_futures
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_futures
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_lender
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_lender
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_railway
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_railway
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_floor
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_floor
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_sound
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_sound
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_pockets
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_pockets }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_pockets
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_has_door
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_door }
				}
				remove_variable = spec_has_door
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_pw_sum }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_price value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_harbour value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_companies value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_futures value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_lender value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_railway value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_floor value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_sound value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_pockets value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_door value = 0 }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at value = 0 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price var:spec_pu_price = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_price = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_price value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_price value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour var:spec_pu_harbour = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_harbour = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_harbour value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies var:spec_pu_companies = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_companies = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_companies value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures var:spec_pu_futures = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_futures = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_futures value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender var:spec_pu_lender = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_lender = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_lender value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_railway = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_railway value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_floor = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_floor value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_sound = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_sound value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_pockets var:spec_pu_pockets = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_pockets = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_pockets value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_pockets value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_door var:spec_pu_door = 1 local_var:spec_pu_d_door = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_door value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_door value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price var:spec_pu_price = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_price = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_price value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_price value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour var:spec_pu_harbour = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_harbour = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_harbour value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_harbour value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies var:spec_pu_companies = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_companies = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_companies value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_companies value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures var:spec_pu_futures = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_futures = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_futures value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_futures value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender var:spec_pu_lender = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_lender = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_lender value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_lender value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway var:spec_pu_railway = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_railway = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_railway value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_railway value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_floor var:spec_pu_floor = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_floor = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_floor value = local_var:spec_pu_at }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_d_floor value = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_sound var:spec_pu_sound = 2 local_var:spec_pu_d_sound = 0 }
					change_local_variable = { name = spec_pu_at add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_pu_sound value = local_var:spec_pu_at }

		limit = { has_variable = spec_xs_regtop }

	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = spec_xs_rlead_1 }
		remove_global_variable = spec_xs_rlead_1
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_rbest_1 value = 0 }
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_centre_score
			var:spec_xs_reg = 1
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order
		position = 0
		check_range_bounds = no
		set_variable = spec_xs_regtop
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_rbest_1 value = var:spec_xs_raw }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_rlead_1 value = this }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = spec_xs_rlead_2 }
		remove_global_variable = spec_xs_rlead_2
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_rbest_2 value = 0 }
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_centre_score
			var:spec_xs_reg = 2
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order

			var:spec_xs_reg = 5
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order

		set_global_variable = { name = spec_xs_rlead_6 value = this }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_centre_score }
		set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 1
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_1 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 2
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_2 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_2 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 3
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_3 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_3 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 4
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_4 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_4 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 5
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_5 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:spec_xs_reg = 6
				global_var:spec_xs_rbest_6 > 0
			}
			set_variable = { name = spec_xs_regshare value = { value = var:spec_xs_raw multiply = 100 divide = global_var:spec_xs_rbest_6 } }
		}
	}
	# the Exchange's Standing (centres.py)
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = spec_centre_score
				has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_score } }
			remove_variable = spec_exch_stand
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_price }
				remove_variable = spec_lead_price
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_harbour }
				remove_variable = spec_lead_harbour
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_companies }
				remove_variable = spec_lead_companies
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_futures }
				remove_variable = spec_lead_futures

			if = {

				remove_variable = spec_lead_lender

				remove_variable = spec_lead_sound

				remove_variable = spec_lead_pockets
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_door }
				remove_variable = spec_lead_door
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_modifier = spec_exch_national }
				remove_modifier = spec_exch_national
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_modifier = spec_exch_world }
				remove_modifier = spec_exch_world
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_local_variable = { name = spec_exch_was value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand }
				set_local_variable = { name = spec_exch_was value = var:spec_exch_stand }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = spec_centre_new
					var:spec_centre_new <= 3
					has_technology_researched = central_banking
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_stand value = 3 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_variable = spec_centre_new
						has_variable = spec_exch_hold  # ponytail: a year's grace, so places 5 and 6 swapping does not flip it monthly
						has_variable = spec_xs_regtop
					}
					has_technology_researched = stock_exchange
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_stand value = 2 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_variable = spec_centre_new
						var:spec_centre_score >= 15
						var:spec_xs_regshare >= 35
						AND = {
							has_variable = spec_exch_stand
							var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
							OR = {
								var:spec_centre_score >= 13
								var:spec_xs_regshare >= 30
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_stand value = 1 }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_stand value = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_crunch }
				if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = { value = 1 multiply = spec_exch_loss } min = 0 } }
				if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_debts_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
				if = {
					limit = { var:spec_exch_stand > local_var:spec_exch_was }
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_stand value = local_var:spec_exch_was }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_variable = spec_centre_new
						has_variable = spec_xs_regtop
					}
				}
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_hold days = 365 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
					NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_new }  # a listed place has its own prestige
					NOT = { has_modifier = spec_exch_national }
				}
				add_modifier = { name = spec_exch_national }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:spec_exch_stand = 3
					NOT = { has_modifier = spec_exch_world }
				}
				add_modifier = { name = spec_exch_world }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					var:spec_exch_stand < 3
					has_modifier = spec_exch_world
				}
				remove_modifier = spec_exch_world
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						var:spec_exch_stand = 0
						has_variable = spec_centre_new
					}
					has_modifier = spec_exch_national
				}
				remove_modifier = spec_exch_national
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_player = yes
					local_var:spec_exch_was < var:spec_exch_stand
				}
				post_notification = spec_exch_up
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_player = yes
					local_var:spec_exch_was > var:spec_exch_stand
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_price }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_price
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_harbour }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_harbour
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_companies }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_companies
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_futures }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_futures
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_lender }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_lender
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_railway }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_railway
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_floor }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_floor
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_sound }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_sound
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_pockets }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_pockets
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lost_door }
					remove_variable = spec_pu_lost_door
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_pursue_n }
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_price
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_price = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_price
						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_harbour
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_harbour = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_harbour
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_companies
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_companies = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_companies
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_futures
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_futures = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_futures
						spec_pursue_stop_futures = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_lender
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_lender = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_lender
						spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_variable = spec_pu_railway
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

					if = {

							has_variable = spec_pu_floor
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

							has_variable = spec_pu_sound

							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

					}

						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}

							var:spec_pu_harbour = var:spec_pursue_n

						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_harbour
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}

							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_companies = var:spec_pursue_n

						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_companies
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {

							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

					}
					if = {

					}
					if = {

							has_variable = spec_pu_futures
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

					}
					if = {

							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots

						spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
					}

						spec_pursue_stop_floor = yes
					}

							has_variable = spec_pu_sound
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_sound = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_sound
						spec_pursue_stop_sound = yes
					}
					if = {

							has_variable = spec_pu_pockets
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_pockets = var:spec_pursue_n
						}

						spec_pursue_stop_pockets = yes
					}

							has_variable = spec_pu_door
							var:spec_pursue_n > spec_pursue_slots
							var:spec_pu_door = var:spec_pursue_n
						}
						set_variable = spec_pu_lost_door
						spec_pursue_stop_door = yes
					}
				}
				post_notification = spec_exch_down
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_player = no OR = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand } var:spec_exch_stand < 1 } NOT = { has_variable = spec_exch_tally } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_exch_tally days = 365 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						spec_earn_tc >= 1
						spec_earn_tc > spec_earn_snd
						spec_earn_tc > spec_earn_co
						NOT = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 }
					}
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 1 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
						spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
						spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						spec_earn_snd >= 1
						spec_earn_snd > spec_earn_tc
						spec_earn_snd > spec_earn_co
						NOT = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 }
					}
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 2 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures }
						spec_pursue_stop_futures = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
						spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						spec_earn_co >= 1
						spec_earn_co > spec_earn_tc
						spec_earn_co > spec_earn_snd
						has_technology_researched = stock_exchange
						NOT = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 }
					}
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 3 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures }
						spec_pursue_stop_futures = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
						spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# once a year (centres.py): the AI's charters and specialities
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_variable = spec_names_year }
			any_country = {
				has_variable = spec_exch_stand
				var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
			}
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_names_year days = 365 }
		# the AI spreads its charters so each charter path has its three places (centres.py)
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_1 value = 0 }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_2 value = 0 }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_3 value = 0 }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } }
			change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_1 add = 1 }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } }
			change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_2 add = 1 }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } }
			change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_3 add = 1 }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { is_player = no has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_1 <= global_var:spec_chn_2 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_1 <= global_var:spec_chn_3 }
						OR = {
							NOR = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_2 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_1 < 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 NOT = { AND = { has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_3 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_1 < 3 }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_2 add = -1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_3 add = -1 }
					}
					change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_1 add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 1 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
						spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
						spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_2 <= global_var:spec_chn_1 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_2 <= global_var:spec_chn_3 }
						OR = {
							NOR = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_1 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_2 < 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 NOT = { AND = { has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_3 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_2 < 3 }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_1 add = -1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_3 add = -1 }
					}
					change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_2 add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 2 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_companies }
						spec_pursue_stop_companies = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures }
						spec_pursue_stop_futures = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_railway }
						spec_pursue_stop_railway = yes
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_technology_researched = stock_exchange OR = { spec_name_companies > 0 spec_name_railway > 0 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_3 <= global_var:spec_chn_1 } OR = { NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } } } global_var:spec_chn_3 <= global_var:spec_chn_2 }
						OR = {
							NOR = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 var:spec_exch_known ?= 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_1 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_3 < 3 }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 NOT = { AND = { OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } } } }
							AND = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } global_var:spec_chn_2 > 3 global_var:spec_chn_3 < 3 }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 OR = { spec_name_price > 0 spec_name_futures > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_1 add = -1 }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 OR = { spec_name_harbour > 0 spec_name_lender > 0 } }
						change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_2 add = -1 }
					}
					change_global_variable = { name = spec_chn_3 add = 1 }
					set_variable = { name = spec_exch_known value = 3 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price }
						spec_pursue_stop_price = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour }
						spec_pursue_stop_harbour = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_futures }
						spec_pursue_stop_futures = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_lender }
						spec_pursue_stop_lender = yes
					}
				}
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_price value = { value = 0 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 has_variable = spec_pu_price } add = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_price }
			remove_global_variable = spec_bestv_price
		}
		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				is_country_alive = yes
				has_variable = spec_exch_stand
				var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
				var:spec_exch_known ?= 1
				spec_name_price > 0
			}
			order_by = spec_name_price
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_bestv_price value = { value = spec_name_price min = 0.01 } }
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_harbour value = { value = 0 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 has_variable = spec_pu_harbour } add = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_harbour }
			remove_global_variable = spec_bestv_harbour
		}
		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				is_country_alive = yes
				has_variable = spec_exch_stand
				var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
				var:spec_exch_known ?= 2
				spec_name_harbour > 0
			}
			order_by = spec_name_harbour
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_bestv_harbour value = { value = spec_name_harbour min = 0.01 } }
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_companies value = { value = 0 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 has_variable = spec_pu_companies } add = 1 } } }
		if = {

		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				is_country_alive = yes

			}
			order_by = spec_name_companies
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no

		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_floor }

				is_country_alive = yes

			}
			order_by = spec_name_floor

			set_global_variable = { name = spec_bestv_floor value = { value = spec_name_floor min = 0.01 } }

		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_sound }
			remove_global_variable = spec_bestv_sound
		}
		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				is_country_alive = yes

			}

		set_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_pockets value = { value = 0 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 has_variable = spec_pu_pockets } add = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_pockets }
			remove_global_variable = spec_bestv_pockets

			order_by = spec_name_pockets

		set_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_door value = { value = 0 every_country = { limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 has_variable = spec_pu_door } add = 1 } } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_door }
			remove_global_variable = spec_bestv_door
		}
		ordered_country = {
			limit = {
				is_country_alive = yes
				has_variable = spec_exch_stand
				var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
				spec_name_door > 0
			}
			order_by = spec_name_door
			position = 0
			check_range_bounds = no
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_bestv_door value = { value = spec_name_door min = 0.01 } }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_exch_stand }
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_player = no
					has_variable = spec_exch_stand
				}
				if = {
					limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 NOT = { OR = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_pursue_n } spec_pursue_slots > var:spec_pursue_n } } }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_worst value = 99 }
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_price NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_lock_price } }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_price }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_price divide = global_var:spec_ldv_price max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_price }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_price divide = global_var:spec_bestv_price max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_m divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_price subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_m < local_var:spec_ai_worst }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_worst value = local_var:spec_ai_m }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_drop value = 0 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_price } var:spec_exch_known ?= 1 }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_price }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_price divide = global_var:spec_ldv_price max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_price }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_price divide = global_var:spec_bestv_price max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_m divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_price multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_m > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_m }
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_lock_harbour } }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_harbour }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_harbour divide = global_var:spec_ldv_harbour max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_harbour }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_harbour divide = global_var:spec_bestv_harbour max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_m divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_harbour subtract = 1 multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_m < local_var:spec_ai_worst }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_worst value = local_var:spec_ai_m }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_drop value = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_pu_harbour } var:spec_exch_known ?= 2 }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_harbour }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_m value = { value = spec_name_harbour divide = global_var:spec_ldv_harbour max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_harbour }

						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_futures value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_futures divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_futures multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_futures > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_futures }
						}
					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_lender value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_lender = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_lender value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_lender }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_lender value = { value = spec_name_lender divide = global_var:spec_ldv_lender max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_lender }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_lender value = { value = spec_name_lender divide = global_var:spec_bestv_lender max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_lender value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_lender divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_lender multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_lender > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_lender }
						}
					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_railway value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_railway = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_railway value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_railway }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_railway value = { value = spec_name_railway divide = global_var:spec_ldv_railway max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_railway }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_railway value = { value = spec_name_railway divide = global_var:spec_bestv_railway max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_railway value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_railway divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_railway multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_railway > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_railway }
						}
					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_floor value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_floor = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_floor value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_floor }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_floor value = { value = spec_name_floor divide = global_var:spec_ldv_floor max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_floor }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_floor value = { value = spec_name_floor divide = global_var:spec_bestv_floor max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_floor value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_floor divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_floor multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_floor > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_floor }
						}
					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_sound value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_sound = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_sound value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_sound }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_sound value = { value = spec_name_sound divide = global_var:spec_ldv_sound max = 1.5 } }

						}

					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_pockets value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_pockets = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_pockets value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_pockets }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_pockets value = { value = spec_name_pockets divide = global_var:spec_ldv_pockets max = 1.5 } }
						}
						else_if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_bestv_pockets }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_pockets value = { value = spec_name_pockets divide = global_var:spec_bestv_pockets max = 1.5 } }
						}
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_pockets value = { value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_pockets divide = { value = global_var:spec_pun_pockets multiply = 0.25 add = 1 } } }
						if = {
							limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_pockets > local_var:spec_ai_best }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_best value = local_var:spec_ai_sh_pockets }
						}
					}
					set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_door value = -1 }
					if = {
						limit = { spec_can_pursue_door = yes }
						set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_door value = 0 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_global_variable = spec_ldv_door }
							set_local_variable = { name = spec_ai_sh_door value = { value = spec_name_door divide = global_var:spec_ldv_door max = 1.5 } }
						}

						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_price >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_price = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_price = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_price add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_harbour >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_harbour = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_harbour = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_harbour add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_companies >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_companies = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_companies = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_companies add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_futures >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_futures = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_futures = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_futures add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_lender >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_lender = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_lender = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_lender add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_railway >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_railway = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_railway = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_railway add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_floor >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_floor = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_floor = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_floor add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_sound >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_sound = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_sound = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_sound add = 1 }
						}
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { local_var:spec_ai_sh_pockets >= 0 local_var:spec_ai_sh_pockets = local_var:spec_ai_best }
						spec_pursue_to_pockets = yes
						if = {
							limit = { var:spec_exch_stand >= 1 }
							change_global_variable = { name = spec_pun_pockets add = 1 }

						spec_pursue_to_door = yes

			}
		}

	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_lead_price }
		set_variable = { name = spec_ldw_price value = var:spec_lead_price }
		remove_variable = spec_lead_price
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = spec_w_price1 }
		remove_global_variable = spec_w_price1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = spec_w_price2 }
		remove_global_variable = spec_w_price2
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable = spec_w_price3 }
		remove_global_variable = spec_w_price3
	}
	set_global_variable = { name = spec_name_next value = 1 }
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			is_country_alive = yes
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
			var:spec_exch_known ?= 1
			has_variable = spec_pu_price
			spec_name_price > 0
		}
		order_by = spec_rank_price
		max = 3
		check_range_bounds = no
		set_variable = { name = spec_lead_price value = global_var:spec_name_next }
		if = {
			limit = { global_var:spec_name_next = 1 }
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_w_price1 value = this }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { global_var:spec_name_next = 2 }
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_w_price2 value = this }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { global_var:spec_name_next = 3 }
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_w_price3 value = this }
		}
		change_global_variable = { name = spec_name_next add = 1 }
	}

	# rival Exchanges (rivals.py): an old save's single named rival becomes a named rival; the rest of its state goes
	every_country = {
		limit = { OR = { has_variable = spec_rival has_variable = spec_rival_lock has_variable = spec_rival_last has_variable = spec_rv_shored has_variable = spec_rv_shore_cd } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = var:spec_rival
				var:spec_rival = { is_country_alive = yes }
				NOT = { this = var:spec_rival }
			}
			var:spec_rival = { save_scope_as = target }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_target_in_variable_list = { name = spec_rv_chosen target = scope:target } } }
				add_to_variable_list = { name = spec_rv_chosen target = scope:target }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_rival }
			remove_variable = spec_rival
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_rival_lock }
			remove_variable = spec_rival_lock
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_rival_last }
			remove_variable = spec_rival_last
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_rv_shored }
			remove_variable = spec_rv_shored
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_rv_shore_cd }
			remove_variable = spec_rv_shore_cd
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = spec_rv_round } }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_rv_round value = 0 }
	}

		spec_rv_count = yes

	if = {

	# monthly: every contest's clock moves a month, and those at ROUND years settle (spec_rv_epoch_day: when, for the countdowns)
	if = {

		spec_rival_contest = yes

		set_global_variable = { name = spec_rival_year days = 365 }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_rv_epoch_year value = year }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_rv_epoch_month value = { value = month add = 1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = spec_rival_started } }
			set_global_variable = { name = spec_rv_epoch_day value = game_date }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_variable = spec_rival_started }

		set_global_variable = spec_rival_started

	# blue chips (bluechips.py): once a year their dividend in trust, then Company of the Year
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_variable = spec_coty_year } }
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_coty_year days = 365 }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_centre_trust has_variable = spec_bc_n var:spec_bc_n > 0 }
			if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
			set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust add = { value = spec_bc_counted multiply = 0.5 } max = 120 } }
			if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_chips_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
		}
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_coty_p value = 0 }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_bc_n var:spec_bc_n > 0 }
			every_company = {
				limit = { has_variable = spec_blue_chip }
				remove_variable = spec_coty
				if = {
					limit = { prosperity > global_var:spec_coty_p }
					set_global_variable = { name = spec_coty_p value = prosperity }
					save_scope_as = spec_coty_co
				}
			}
		}
		scope:spec_coty_co ?= {
			set_variable = { name = spec_coty days = 365 }
			owner = {
				add_modifier = { name = spec_coty days = 365 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = spec_centre_trust }
					if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } set_local_variable = { name = spec_before_trust value = var:spec_centre_trust } }
					set_variable = { name = spec_centre_trust value = { value = var:spec_centre_trust add = 3 max = 120 } }
					if = { limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_trust_review } change_variable = { name = spec_trust_chips_pending add = { value = var:spec_centre_trust subtract = local_var:spec_before_trust } } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes }
					trigger_event = { id = spec_bluechip.3 popup = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_global_variable = spec_coty_p
	}

		set_global_variable = { name = spec_centre_25 value = this }
	}
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
			NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_pos }
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order
		position = 0
		check_range_bounds = no
		set_variable = spec_centre_pos
		set_variable = spec_centre_also
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_centre_26 value = this }
	}
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
			NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_pos }
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order

		check_range_bounds = no
		set_variable = spec_centre_pos
		set_variable = spec_centre_also
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_centre_27 value = this }

		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand

	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			var:spec_exch_stand >= 1
			NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_pos }

		order_by = spec_centre_order

		set_variable = spec_centre_pos
		set_variable = spec_centre_also
		set_global_variable = { name = spec_centre_40 value = this }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_centre_pos }
		remove_variable = spec_centre_pos
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_variable_list = spec_exch_prov }
		clear_global_variable_list = spec_exch_prov
	}
	ordered_country = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = spec_exch_stand
			var:spec_exch_stand = 0
		}
		order_by = spec_centre_order
		max = 300
		check_range_bounds = no
		add_to_global_variable_list = { name = spec_exch_prov target = this }
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { is_player = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable_list = spec_exch_prov }
			clear_variable_list = spec_exch_prov
		}
		save_scope_as = spec_prov_player
		every_in_global_list = {
			variable = spec_exch_prov
			scope:spec_prov_player = {
				add_to_variable_list = { name = spec_exch_prov target = prev }
			}
		}
	}
	# ponytail: a market hovering between the last place and the next gets a toast each time it crosses; hold a place
	# for a few months if that proves noisy
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_variable = spec_centre_rank
				has_variable = spec_centre_new
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_new } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_modifier = spec_financial_centre }
				remove_modifier = spec_financial_centre
			}
			remove_variable = spec_centre_rank
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = spec_centre_rank } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_rank value = 0 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { var:spec_centre_rank = var:spec_centre_new } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_modifier = spec_financial_centre }
					remove_modifier = spec_financial_centre
				}
				add_modifier = { name = spec_financial_centre multiplier = spec_centre_mult }
				set_variable = { name = spec_centre_rank value = var:spec_centre_new }
			}
			remove_variable = spec_centre_new
		}
	}
}
